## 👤 User(sidechain) · 2026-08-13T08:58:28.697Z

```
你是泰拉瑞亚 1.4.5.6 复刻项目的世界生成审计员。我们的复刻在 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/src/world/gen/vanilla/ 下;唯一标杆是反编译真源 ~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/(C#,目录名如 Terraria.GameContent.Generation.Dungeon 等,大方法在 Terraria/WorldGen.cs)。1405 目录仅交叉参照,冲突时 1456 为准。

【任务】逐文件深读以下结构类 pass,对照 1456 对应源码,找出**仍然活跃的"自制机制"**(即非原版逻辑、会改变生成结果的发明),不是找注释风格问题:

文件清单(每个都要通读):
- TemplePass.ts(丛林神庙)
- JungleShrinePass.ts(丛林神龛)
- LivingTreePass.ts(生命树)
- IslandHousePass.ts(浮空岛)
- CaveHousePass.ts(洞穴小屋)
- HellFortPass.ts(地狱废墟/塔)
- HiveSpiderPass.ts(蜂巢+蜘蛛巢——已知线索 :240 "结构保护图近似(无全局 StructureMap):主巢 ±50(padding 5)与蜂蜜斑 16×16 互斥"、:409/:597 PlaceTile 近似)
- MultitileFragmentSweep.ts(残片清扫)

【判定目标类(按严重度)】
1. 自制锚点扫描/吸附(如"找首个实心再吸附",原版直接用传入坐标)
2. 自制兜底/强制路径(原版失败就放弃,我们却补挖/补放/强制放置)
3. 自制钳制/回拉/方向强制(原版没有的漂移修正)
4. 循环上限/重试次数与原版不同(如原版无上限我们加了 cap,或反之)
5. 掷骰流偏差:循环里多耗/少耗 RNG(只报"每 pass 常态性的多耗",个别常量差忽略)
6. 共享值重新掷骰(原版一次掷出全程共用,我们重复掷)
7. 顺序偏差(该 pass 内部操作序与原版不同且影响结果)

【不报】(已备案口径,逐条标了 1456 行号的静态查表近似,如 tileSolidTop≈platform、帧号近似、cosmetic 差异)——除非你能证明该近似实际上错了。

【输出格式】每条:
- 文件:行号 + 一句话机制描述
- 原版权威出处(1456 文件:行号 + 原版行为一句话)
- 影响分级:geometry(改变地形/结构)/ rng-stream(只错开掷骰流)/ cosmetic
- 裁决:must-fix(原版行为明确且我们的发明会错)/ acceptable(发明但无害或有意的引擎差异)/ uncertain(需人工定夺)
最后给一段总体结论。只输出确认的发现,宁缺毋滥;引用原版时必须先打开对应源码核实,禁止凭记忆。只读审计,禁止修改任何文件。
```

25	const MUD = TILE_BY_KEY['mud']!;

27	const BAD_TILES = new Set([sheetTile(225), sheetTile(229), TILE_BY_KEY['v_226_lihzahrd_brick']!,

30	/** JungleShrines pass:返回神龛中心列表(供后续常春藤箱 pass 消费) */
31	export function runJungleShrinePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): Array<{ x: number; y: number }> {
32	  const { w, h } = st;
33	  const spots: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> = [];
34	  // jungleHut 每世界五选一——值来自 Reset 掷(cs:11182 Next(5) → cs:11333-11352 映射),
35	  // 此处复用 gs.jungleHut 不再重掷(曾重掷致流位+样式双偏)
36	  const hutIdx = gs.jungleHut;
37	  const hutTile = HUT_TILES[hutIdx];
38	  if (hutTile === undefined || hutTile < 0) return spots;
39	  const hutWall = HUT_WALLS[hutIdx];
40	
41	  // cs:16065-16068:num3 = Next(7,12) × w/4200(裸 double),循环条件是浮点比较
42	  //   i < num3——非整数倍时按 ceil 计(如 6300 宽 7×1.5=10.5 → 11 座,勿 floor)
43	  const num3 = rng.int(7, 11) * (w / 4200);
44	  let x = rng.int(40, w - 41);
45	  let y = rng.int(Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2), h - 401);
46	  let retries = 0;
47	  for (let i = 0; i < num3; i++) {
48	    let placed = false;
49	    while (!placed) {
50	      retries++;
51	      // 地牢异侧半区(cs:16071-16076)
52	      x = rng.int(40, Math.floor(w / 2) - 41);
53	      if (gs.dungeonSide <= 0) x += Math.floor(w / 2);
54	      y = rng.int(Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2), h - 401);
55	      const halfW = rng.int(2, 3);  // num5
56	      const halfH = rng.int(2, 3);  // num6
57	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y) || !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] || st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== JUNGLE_GRASS) continue;
58	      // ±30 步进 3 排除扫描(cs:16088-16102)
59	      let bad = false;
60	      for (let jx = x - 30; jx < x + 30 && !bad; jx += 3) {
61	        for (let jy = y - 30; jy < y + 30 && !bad; jy += 3) {
62	          if (!st.inBounds(jx, jy)) continue;
63	          if (st.flags[st.idx(jx, jy)] && BAD_TILES.has(st.type[st.idx(jx, jy)])) bad = true;
64	          const wl = st.wall[st.idx(jx, jy)];
65	          if (wl === 86 || wl === 87) bad = true;
66	        }
67	      }
68	      if (bad) {
69	        if (retries > w * 10) { retries = 0; break; } // 放弃本座(cs:16205)
70	        continue;
71	      }
72	      // 实体壳(cs:16116-16127)
73	      for (let lx = x - halfW - 1; lx <= x + halfW + 1; lx++) {
74	        for (let ly = y - halfH - 1; ly <= y + halfH + 1; ly++) {
75	          if (!st.inBounds(lx, ly)) continue;
76	          st.setTileSilent(lx, ly, hutTile);
77	          st.liquid[st.idx(lx, ly)] = 0;
78	          st.liquidType[st.idx(lx, ly)] = 0;
79	        }
80	      }
81	      // 空腔 + 墙(cs:16129-16137)
82	      for (let lx = x - halfW; lx <= x + halfW; lx++) {
83	        for (let ly = y - halfH; ly <= y + halfH; ly++) {
84	          if (!st.inBounds(lx, ly)) continue;
85	          st.setTileSilent(lx, ly, 0);
86	          st.setWallSilent(lx, ly, hutWall);
87	        }
88	      }
89	      // 腔内火把(样式 3 丛林火把,cs:16152-16154)。★样式在 Y 轴(PlaceTile case4
90	      //   cs:59863 frameY=22*style,与 StructuresPass.placeTorch 同);y 上界
91	      //   cs:16153 Next(num2-num6, num2+num6-2) 不含上界
92	      for (let t = 0; t < 100; t++) {
93	        const tx = rng.int(x - halfW, x + halfW);
94	        const ty = rng.int(y - halfH, y + halfH - 3);
95	        if (!st.flags[st.idx(tx, ty)]) {
96	          st.setTileSilent(tx, ty, TORCH, 0, 3 * 22);
97	          break;
98	        }
99	      }
100	      // 底部入口:掏空壳底两行(cs:16152-16162)
101	      for (let lx = x - halfW - 1; lx <= x + halfW + 1; lx++) {
102	        for (let ly = y + halfH - 2; ly <= y + halfH; ly++) {
103	          if (st.inBounds(lx, ly)) st.setTileSilent(lx, ly, 0);
104	        }
105	      }
106	      // 泥地基:壳底向下最多 4 格填泥(cs:16164-16174)
107	      for (let lx = x - halfW - 1; lx <= x + halfW + 1; lx++) {
108	        let fill = 4;
109	        let ly = y + halfH + 2;
110	        while (ly < h && !st.flags[st.idx(lx, ly)] && fill > 0) {
111	          st.setTileSilent(lx, ly, MUD);
112	          ly++; fill--;
113	        }
114	      }
115	      // 金字塔顶(cs:16176-16194)
116	      let topHalf = halfW - rng.int(1, 2);
117	      let ty = y - halfH - 2;
118	      while (topHalf > -1) {
119	        for (let lx = x - topHalf - 1; lx <= x + topHalf + 1; lx++) {
120	          if (st.inBounds(lx, ty)) st.setTileSilent(lx, ty, hutTile);
121	        }
122	        topHalf -= rng.int(1, 2);
123	        ty--;
124	      }
125	      spots.push({ x, y });
126	      retries = 0;
127	      placed = true;
128	    }
129	  }
130	  return spots;
131	}
132	
133	/** ChestsInJungleShrines pass(cs:17308):神龛中心放常春藤箱;失败掏 3×3 后原地重试 */
134	export function runJungleChestsPass(
135	  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink, spots: Array<{ x: number; y: number }>,
136	): void {
137	  for (const s of spots) {
138	    const main = nextJungleItem(gs, rng);
139	    if (!placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, s.x + rng.int(0, 1), s.y, { style: 10, loot: main, flags: { ivy: true } })) {
140	      for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
141	        for (let dy = 0; dy <= 2; dy++) {
142	          if (st.inBounds(s.x + dx, s.y + dy)) st.setTileSilent(s.x + dx, s.y + dy, 0);
143	        }
144	      }
145	      placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, s.x, s.y, { style: 10, loot: main, flags: { ivy: true } });
146	    }
147	  }
148	}

14	const ALTAR = TILE_BY_KEY['v_237_lihzahrd_altar']!;
15	const JUNGLE_GRASS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']!;

17	interface Room { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }
18	
19	function inter(a: Room, b: Room): boolean {
20	  // C# Rectangle.Intersects:仅边接触不算相交
21	  return a.x < b.x + b.w && b.x < a.x + a.w && a.y < b.y + b.h && b.y < a.y + a.h;
22	}
23	
24	function setBrick(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): void {
25	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
26	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
27	  st.type[i] = BRICK; st.flags[i] = 1; st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0;
28	}
29	
30	export function runTemplePass(
31	  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState,
32	  chests: Array<{ x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null> }>,
33	): void {
34	  const { w, h } = st;
35	  const s = w / 4200;
36	  // 种子点(cs:15917-15992):y = Next(rockLayer, maxTilesY-600)(rockLayer 先钳 ≤ h-601);
37	  // x = ((NextDouble()*num3+0.1)*-dungeonSide+0.5)*w,num3 起 0.25,每 1e6 次失败
38	  // num3 = min(0.35, num3+0.05) 递增(cs:15978-15989);落点须为丛林草 60(cs:15972)。
39	  // num3==0.35 后再失败 10 轮放弃(不作原版地表补救 cs:15995+,直接返回)。
40	  let sx = -1, sy = -1;
41	  {
42	    let band = 0.25, fails = 0, giveups = 0;
43	    for (;;) {
44	      let yTop = Math.floor(gs.rockLevel);
45	      const yBot = h - 600;
46	      if (yTop > yBot - 1) yTop = yBot - 1;
47	      const y = rng.int(yTop, yBot - 1);
48	      const x = Math.floor(((rng.next() * band + 0.1) * -gs.dungeonSide + 0.5) * w);
49	      if (st.inBounds(x, y)) {
50	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
51	        if (st.flags[i] && st.type[i] === JUNGLE_GRASS) { sx = x; sy = y; break; }
52	      }
53	      if (fails++ > 1000000) {
54	        if (band === 0.35 && ++giveups > 10) break;
55	        band = Math.min(0.35, band + 0.05);
56	        fails = 0;
57	      }
58	    }
59	  }
60	  if (sx < 0) return;

67	  let runStep = 0;                        // num9
68	  let cx = sx, cy = sy;                   // num6/num7:上一间中心
69	  let overlap = -10;                      // num13
70	  for (let n = 0; n < roomCount; n++) {

179	        if (l > midX) l = midX;
180	        if (rr < midX) rr = midX;
181	        if (t > midY) t = midY;
182	        if (b < midY) b = midY;
183	        if (x >= l && x < rr && y >= t && y <= b) carveCell(x, y);
184	      }
185	    }
186	  }
187	  // ---- templePather 通道(L17392-17458):从种子游走穿各房,方块清空+墙 87 ----
188	  const pather = (px: number, py: number, destX: number, destY: number): [number, number] => {
189	    let x = px, y = py;
190	    let steps = rng.int(5, 19);
191	    const rad = rng.int(2, 4);
192	    while (steps > 0 && (x !== destX || y !== destY)) {
193	      steps--;
194	      if (x > destX) x--;
195	      if (x < destX) x++;
196	      if (y > destY) y--;
197	      if (y < destY) y++;
198	      for (let ix = x - rad; ix < x + rad; ix++) {
199	        for (let iy = y - rad; iy < y + rad; iy++) {
200	          if (!st.inBounds(ix, iy)) continue;
201	          const i = st.idx(ix, iy);
202	          st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0; st.wall[i] = 87;
203	        }
204	      }
205	    }
206	    return [x, y];
207	  };
208	  let tpx = sx, tpy = sy;
209	  for (let n = 0; n < rooms.length; n++) {
210	    const r = rooms[n];
211	    const inner: Room = { x: r.x + 8, y: r.y + 8, w: Math.max(1, r.w - 16), h: Math.max(1, r.h - 16) };
212	    for (;;) {
213	      let destX: number, destY: number;
214	      if (n === rooms.length - 1) {
215	        destX = inner.x + Math.floor(inner.w / 2) + rng.int(-10, 9);
216	        destY = inner.y + Math.floor(inner.h / 2) + rng.int(-10, 9);
217	      } else {
218	        destX = rng.int(inner.x, inner.x + inner.w - 1);
219	        destY = rng.int(inner.y, inner.y + inner.h - 1);
220	      }
221	      [tpx, tpy] = pather(tpx, tpy, destX, destY);
222	      if (tpx === destX && tpy === destY) break;
223	    }
224	    // 通向下一间的附加通道(2/3 概率近旁绕行,1/3 中点)
225	    if (n < rooms.length - 1) {
226	      const a = rooms[n], b2 = rooms[n + 1];
227	      let tx: number, ty: number;
228	      if (rng.int(0, 2) !== 0) {
229	        if (b2.y >= a.y + a.h) {
230	          tx = b2.x + (n === 0
231	            ? (startDir > 0 ? Math.floor(b2.w * 0.8) : Math.floor(b2.w * 0.2))
232	            : (b2.x < a.x ? Math.floor(b2.w * 0.2) : Math.floor(b2.w * 0.8)));
233	          ty = b2.y;
234	        } else {
235	          tx = Math.floor((a.x + Math.floor(a.w / 2) + b2.x + Math.floor(b2.w / 2)) / 2);
236	          ty = Math.floor(b2.y + b2.h * 0.8);
237	        }
238	      } else {
239	        tx = Math.floor((a.x + Math.floor(a.w / 2) + b2.x + Math.floor(b2.w / 2)) / 2);
240	        ty = Math.floor((a.y + Math.floor(a.h / 2) + b2.y + Math.floor(b2.h / 2)) / 2);
241	      }
242	      for (;;) {
243	        const destX = rng.int(tx - 6, tx + 6);
244	        const destY = rng.int(ty - 6, ty + 6);
245	        [tpx, tpy] = pather(tpx, tpy, destX, destY);
246	        if (tpx === destX && tpy === destY) break;
247	      }
248	    }
249	  }
250	  // ---- 外壳范围 + outerTempled 四遍扫描(L17461-17499)----
251	  let bx0 = w - 20, bx1 = 20, by0 = h - 20, by1 = 20;
252	  for (const r of rooms) {
253	    if (r.x < bx0) bx0 = r.x;
254	    if (r.x + r.w > bx1) bx1 = r.x + r.w;
255	    if (r.y < by0) by0 = r.y;
256	    if (r.y + r.h > by1) by1 = r.y + r.h;
257	  }
258	  const ox0 = bx0 - 10, ox1 = bx1 + 10, oy0 = by0 - 10, oy1 = by1 + 10;
259	  const outerTempled = (x: number, y: number) => {
260	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
261	    const i0 = st.idx(x, y);
262	    if ((st.flags[i0] && st.type[i0] === BRICK) || st.wall[i0] === 87) return;
263	    for (let ix = x - 6; ix <= x + 6; ix++) {
264	      for (let iy = y - 6; iy <= y + 6; iy++) {
265	        if (!st.inBounds(ix, iy)) continue;
266	        const j = st.idx(ix, iy);
267	        if (!st.flags[j] && st.wall[j] === 87) { setBrick(st, x, y); return; }
268	      }
269	    }
270	  };
271	  for (let x = ox0; x < ox1; x++) for (let y = oy0; y < oy1; y++) outerTempled(x, y);
272	  for (let x = ox1; x >= ox0; x--) for (let y = oy0; y < Math.floor(oy1 / 2); y++) outerTempled(x, y);
273	  for (let y = oy0; y < oy1; y++) for (let x = ox0; x < ox1; x++) outerTempled(x, y);
274	  for (let y = oy1; y >= oy0; y--) for (let x = ox0; x < ox1; x++) outerTempled(x, y);
275	  // ---- 入口隧道(L17500-17527):固定高度、固定间隔下沉,穿出神庙即停 ----
276	  const edir = -startDir;
277	  let ex = sx + 0.0, ey = sy + 0.0;
278	  const tunH = rng.int(2, 4);
279	  const dropEvery = rng.int(9, 13);
280	  let tick = 0;
281	  for (;;) {
282	    tick++;
283	    if (tick >= dropEvery) { tick = 0; ey--; }
284	    ex += edir;
285	    const xi = Math.floor(ex);
286	    if (xi < 1 || xi >= st.w - 1) break;
287	    let inside = false;
288	    for (let y = Math.floor(ey) - tunH; y < ey + tunH; y++) {
289	      if (!st.inBounds(xi, y)) continue;
290	      const i = st.idx(xi, y);
291	      if (st.wall[i] === 87 || (st.flags[i] && st.type[i] === BRICK)) inside = true;
292	      if (st.flags[i] && st.type[i] === BRICK) {
293	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0; st.wall[i] = 87;
294	      }
295	    }
296	    if (!inside) break;
297	  }
298	  // ---- 门框(L17528-17578):柱/洞/门 tile 10 style 11 ----
299	  const i1 = sx;
300	  let jy = sy;
301	  if (!st.inBounds(i1, jy)) return;
302	  while (jy < st.h - 2 && !st.flags[st.idx(i1, jy)]) jy++;
303	  jy -= 4;   // j1
304	  if (jy < 2) return;
305	  let yUp = jy;
306	  while (yUp > 1 && ((st.flags[st.idx(i1, yUp)] && st.type[st.idx(i1, yUp)] === BRICK) || st.wall[st.idx(i1, yUp)] === 87)) yUp--;
307	  const yFrom = yUp + 2;
308	  for (let x = i1 - 1; x <= i1 + 1; x++) {
309	    for (let y = yFrom; y <= jy; y++) setBrick(st, x, y);
310	  }
311	  for (let x = i1 - 4; x <= i1 + 4; x++) {
312	    for (let y = jy - 1; y < jy + 3; y++) {
313	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
314	      const i = st.idx(x, y);
315	      st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0; st.wall[i] = 87;
316	    }
317	  }
318	  for (let x = i1 - 1; x <= i1 + 1; x++) {
319	    for (let y = jy - 5; y <= jy + 8; y++) setBrick(st, x, y);
320	  }
321	  for (let x = i1 - 3; x <= i1 + 3; x++) {
322	    for (let y = jy - 2; y < jy + 3; y++) {
323	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
324	      if (y >= jy || x < sx - 1 || x > sx + 1) {
325	        const i = st.idx(x, y);
326	        st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0; st.wall[i] = 87;
327	      }
328	    }
329	  }
330	  // 原版 makeTemple 尾部（1456 WorldGen.cs:34176）：PlaceTile(i1, num81=jy, 10, style 11)。
331	  // PlaceTile case 10（:59867）条件 B：j+1/j+2 空 + j+3 实心 → PlaceDoor(i, j+1)（:31938，
332	  // 门占 j..j+2）。此处 jy=地板-4：门顶=jy、门底=jy+2，站地板 jy+3。
333	  // 此前放 jy-2 = 整门悬空 2 格（标注实测）
334	  placeDoorClosed(st, i1, jy, 11);
335	  // ---- templeCleaner 两遍(L17579-17588)----
336	  const cleaner = (x: number, y: number) => {
337	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return;
338	    const nbr = (dx: number, dy: number) => {
339	      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy)) return 0;
340	      const j = st.idx(x + dx, y + dy);
341	      return st.flags[j] && st.type[j] === BRICK ? 1 : 0;
342	    };
343	    const num = nbr(1, 0) + nbr(-1, 0) + nbr(0, 1) + nbr(0, -1);
344	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
345	    if (st.flags[i] && st.type[i] === BRICK) {
346	      if (num > 1) return;
347	      st.type[i] = 0; st.flags[i] = 0; st.wall[i] = 87;
348	    } else {
349	      if (st.flags[i] || num !== 3) return;
350	      setBrick(st, x, y);
351	    }
352	  };
353	  for (let x = ox0; x < ox1; x++) for (let y = oy0; y < oy1; y++) cleaner(x, y);
354	  for (let y = oy1; y >= oy0; y--) for (let x = ox1; x >= ox0; x--) cleaner(x, y);
355	  // ---- 封闭格刷墙 87(L17589-17608):3×3 全是砖或墙 87 ----
356	  for (let x = ox0; x < ox1; x++) {
357	    for (let y = oy0; y < oy1; y++) {
358	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
359	      let all = true;
360	      for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1 && all; dx++) {
361	        for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
362	          if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy)) { all = false; break; }
363	          const j = st.idx(x + dx, y + dy);
364	          if (!(st.flags[j] && st.type[j] === BRICK) && st.wall[j] !== 87) { all = false; break; }
365	        }
366	      }
367	      if (all) st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] = 87;
368	    }
369	  }
370	  // ---- 祭坛 237（makeTemple 尾 cs:34241-34315 1:1）：末间中心 ±15 拒绝采样，
371	  //      Place3x2 锚点门（底行下三列 SolidTile2）；1000 次失败走强制兜底 ----
372	  // SolidTile2（cs:70178）：active+tileSolid+slope0+非半砖
373	  const solidTile2 = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
374	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;
375	    const j = st.idx(x, y);
376	    if (!st.flags[j]) return false;
377	    const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[j]];
378	    return !!d && d.solid && st.slope[j] === 0 && !st.half[j];
379	  };
380	  const lastR = rooms[rooms.length - 1];
381	  const acx = lastR.x + Math.floor(lastR.w / 2);
382	  const acy = lastR.y + Math.floor(lastR.h / 2);
383	  let altarX = -1, altarY = -1;
384	  for (let tries = 0; tries < 1000 && altarX < 0; tries++) {
385	    const ax = acx + 15 - rng.int(0, 29);
386	    const ay = acy + 15 - rng.int(0, 29);
387	    if (!st.inBounds(ax, ay) || st.wall[st.idx(ax, ay)] !== 87) continue;
388	    // PlaceTile 语义:落点须在挖空区(墙 87 且非实心)
389	    if (st.flags[st.idx(ax, ay)]) continue;
390	    // Place3x2 空置门（cs:52109-52120）：3×2 全空
391	    let clear = true;
392	    for (let dx = 0; dx <= 2 && clear; dx++) {
393	      for (let dy = 0; dy <= 1; dy++) {
394	        if (!st.inBounds(ax + dx, ay + dy) || st.flags[st.idx(ax + dx, ay + dy)]) { clear = false; break; }
395	      }
396	    }
397	    if (!clear) continue;
398	    // ★ Place3x2 锚点门（cs:52127-52153，default 分支）：底行下三列 SolidTile2
399	    // ——此前缺此门：采样框下方几乎必然是空气 → 祭坛悬空
400	    let anchored = true;
401	    for (let dx = 0; dx <= 2; dx++) {
402	      if (!solidTile2(ax + dx, ay + 2)) { anchored = false; break; }
403	    }
404	    if (!anchored) continue;
405	    for (let dx = 0; dx <= 2; dx++) {
406	      for (let dy = 0; dy <= 1; dy++) {
407	        st.setTileSilent(ax + dx, ay + dy, ALTAR, dx * 18, dy * 18);
408	      }
409	    }
410	    altarX = ax; altarY = ay;
411	  }
412	  // ---- 兜底（cs:34261-34315 1:1）：中心 ±10 抖动 → 向下扫到首个实心 →
413	  //      强制铺 3 格蜥蜴砖地板 → 锚点改地板上一格、x 左移 1 → 清周围 → 强制放置
414	  if (altarX < 0) {
415	    let fx = acx + rng.int(-10, 10);
416	    let fy = acy + rng.int(-10, 10);
417	    while (st.inBounds(fx, fy) && !st.flags[st.idx(fx, fy)]) fy++;
418	    if (st.inBounds(fx, fy)) {
419	      for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
420	        if (!st.inBounds(fx + dx, fy)) continue;
421	        const j = st.idx(fx + dx, fy);
422	        st.type[j] = BRICK; st.flags[j] = 1;
423	        st.slope[j] = 0; st.half[j] = 0; st.liquid[j] = 0;
424	      }
425	      fy -= 2; fx -= 1;   // 底中锚 → 3×2 左上角
426	      if (st.inBounds(fx, fy)) {
427	        for (let dx = -1; dx <= 3; dx++) {
428	          for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {
429	            if (st.inBounds(fx + dx, fy + dy)) {
430	              const j = st.idx(fx + dx, fy + dy);
431	              st.type[j] = 0; st.flags[j] = 0;
432	            }
433	          }
434	        }
435	        for (let dx = 0; dx <= 2; dx++) {
436	          for (let dy = 0; dy <= 1; dy++) {
437	            st.setTileSilent(fx + dx, fy + dy, ALTAR, dx * 18, dy * 18);
438	          }
439	        }
440	        altarX = fx; altarY = fy;
441	      }
442	    }
443	  }
444	  gs.lAltarX = altarX; gs.lAltarY = altarY;
445	  // ---- templePart2（1456 cs:34497-34685）1:1 五段：陷阱 → 宝箱 → 雕像 → 家具 → 挂饰 ----
446	  // 顺序对齐原版：宝箱在尖刺陷阱段**之后**（旧版反序且公式为房数×1.1×(1±25%)）
447	  templePart2(st, rng, gs, chests, rooms);
448	  // 注：LihzahrdAltar 终保 pass（cs:21753-21791）原版是 TileCleanup(21327) 之后、
449	  // MicroBiomes(21785) 之前的独立 pass（Pots/Traps/TileCleanup 可能扰动祭坛后再
450	  // 重放）。2026-08-13 前并入本函数末尾（执行位≈15911），已外提为
451	  // runLihzahrdAltarPass 独立槽位（WorldGen.ts「蜥蜴祭坛」槽）。
452	}
453	
454	/** LihzahrdAltar 终保 pass（cs:21753-21791 1:1）：重放祭坛 6 格 + 正下方三格
455	 *  强制 226 地板（slope/halfBrick/liquid 复位）——原版注册序在 TileCleanup(21327)
456	 *  之后，对中途 Pots/Traps/TileCleanup 的祭坛扰动做最终回滚（顺带达成
457	 *  cs:21744-21746 的 232→226；TileCleanupPass ⑪ 段同效双保险）。 */
458	export function runLihzahrdAltarPass(st: TileStore, gs: GenState): void {
459	  const altarX = gs.lAltarX, altarY = gs.lAltarY;
460	  if (altarX < 0) return;   // 无神庙世界（Skyblock 等）跳过
461	  for (let dx = 0; dx <= 2; dx++) {
462	    for (let dy = 0; dy <= 1; dy++) {
463	      if (st.inBounds(altarX + dx, altarY + dy)) {
464	        st.setTileSilent(altarX + dx, altarY + dy, ALTAR, dx * 18, dy * 18);
465	      }
466	    }
467	    const bj = st.inBounds(altarX + dx, altarY + 2) ? st.idx(altarX + dx, altarY + 2) : -1;
468	    if (bj >= 0) {
469	      st.type[bj] = BRICK; st.flags[bj] = 1;
470	      st.slope[bj] = 0; st.half[bj] = 0; st.liquid[bj] = 0;
471	    }
472	  }
473	}
474	
475	/** templePart2（1456 cs:34497-34685）1:1。tRooms=房间数；各段预算公式与
476	 *  失败推进语义逐掷对齐（陷阱段 fails>100 直接扣预算；其余段 fails>10000 break）。 */
477	function templePart2(
478	  st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState,
479	  chests: Array<{ x: number; y: number; items: Array<{ id: number; stack: number } | null> }>,
480	  rooms: Room[],
481	): void {
482	  let tLeft = Infinity, tRight = -Infinity, tTop = Infinity, tBottom = -Infinity;
483	  for (const r of rooms) {
484	    tLeft = Math.min(tLeft, r.x); tRight = Math.max(tRight, r.x + r.w);
485	    tTop = Math.min(tTop, r.y); tBottom = Math.max(tBottom, r.y + r.h);
486	  }
487	  const tRooms = rooms.length;
488	  const lx = Math.floor(tLeft), rx = Math.ceil(tRight) - 1;
489	  const ty = Math.floor(tTop), by = Math.ceil(tBottom) - 1;
490	
491	  // ---- 1. 尖刺陷阱（cs:34497-34519）：预算 = 房数×1.9×(1±15%) ----
492	  {
493	    let budget = tRooms * 1.9 * (1 + rng.int(-15, 15) * 0.01);
494	    let fails = 0;
495	    while (budget > 0) {
496	      const x2 = rng.int(lx, rx);
497	      const y2 = rng.int(ty, by);
498	      if (st.inBounds(x2, y2) && st.wall[st.idx(x2, y2)] === 87 && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, y2)]) {
499	        if (mayanTrap(st, rng, gs, x2, y2)) { budget -= 1; fails = 0; }
500	        else fails++;
501	      } else fails++;
502	      if (fails > 100) { fails = 0; budget -= 1; }   // cs:34517-34518
503	    }
504	  }
505	
506	  // ---- 2. 神庙宝箱（cs:34521-34545）：预算 = 房数×0.35×(1±15%) ----
507	  //  AddBuriedChest(mainItem=1293 蜥蜴能量电池, notNearOtherChests:true, style 16)
508	  {
509	    let budget = tRooms * 0.35 * (1 + rng.int(-15, 15) * 0.01);
510	    let fails = 0;
511	    while (budget > 0) {
512	      const x2 = rng.int(lx, rx);
513	      const y2 = rng.int(ty, by);
514	      // 祭坛 ±3 格回避（原版由保护结构承担；防宝箱贴祭坛抢右键交互）
515	      const nearAltar = gs.lAltarX >= 0 && Math.abs(x2 - gs.lAltarX) <= 3 && Math.abs(y2 - gs.lAltarY) <= 3;
516	      if (!nearAltar && st.inBounds(x2, y2) && st.wall[st.idx(x2, y2)] === 87 && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, y2)]) {
517	        if (placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x2, y2, { style: 16, loot: 'vi_1293_lihzahrd_power_cell' })) {
518	          budget -= 1;
519	          fails = 0;
520	        }
521	      }
522	      fails++;
523	      if (fails > 10000) break;
524	    }
525	  }
526	
527	  // ---- 3. 雕像 105 style43-45（cs:34547-34583）：预算 = 房数×1.25×(1+Next(-25,36)%) ----
528	  {
529	    let budget = tRooms * 1.25 * (1 + rng.int(-25, 35) * 0.01);
530	    let fails = 0;
531	    while (budget > 0) {
532	      fails++;
533	      const x2 = rng.int(lx, rx);
534	      const y2 = rng.int(ty, by);
535	      if (st.inBounds(x2, y2) && st.wall[st.idx(x2, y2)] === 87 && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, y2)]) {
536	        let fy = y2;
537	        while (st.inBounds(x2, fy) && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, fy)]) {
538	          fy++;
539	          if (fy > by) break;
540	        }
541	        fy--;
542	        if (fy <= by) {
543	          const style = rng.int(43, 45);              // Next(43,46)
544	          if (placeStatueTemple(st, x2, fy, style)) budget -= 1;
545	        }
546	      }
547	      if (fails > 10000) break;
548	    }
549	  }
550	
551	  // ---- 4. 蜥蜴家具（cs:34585-34632）：预算 = 房数×1.35×(1+Next(-15,26)%) ----
552	  //  Next(3)（cs:34608-34630 原文配对，勿旋转）：0→工作台 18 style10 /
553	  //  1→桌 14 style9 / 2→椅 15 style12（曾把三对整体错抄一位→神庙出木椅）
554	  {
555	    let budget = tRooms * 1.35 * (1 + rng.int(-15, 25) * 0.01);
556	    let fails = 0;
557	    while (budget > 0) {
558	      fails++;
559	      const x2 = rng.int(lx, rx);
560	      const y2 = rng.int(ty, by);
561	      if (st.inBounds(x2, y2) && st.wall[st.idx(x2, y2)] === 87 && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, y2)]) {
562	        let fy = y2;
563	        while (st.inBounds(x2, fy) && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, fy)]) {
564	          fy++;
565	          if (fy > by) break;
566	        }
567	        fy--;
568	        if (fy <= by) {
569	          const kind = rng.int(0, 2);                 // Next(3)
570	          const spec = kind === 0
571	            ? { tile: TILE_BY_KEY['workbench']!, style: 10 }
572	            : kind === 1
573	              ? { tile: TILE_BY_KEY['table']!, style: 9 }
574	              : { tile: TILE_BY_KEY['chair']!, style: 12 };
575	          if (placeFurn(st, x2, fy, spec.tile, spec.style)) budget -= 1;
576	        }
577	      }
578	      if (fails > 10000) break;
579	    }
580	  }
581	
582	  // ---- 5. 墙挂饰 240 style88（cs:34634-34682）：数量 = 1 + (w>4200) + (w>6400?Next(2):0) ----
583	  {
584	    let count = 1;
585	    if (st.w > 4200) count++;
586	    if (st.w > 6400) count += rng.int(0, 1);
587	    let fails = 0;
588	    while (count > 0) {
589	      fails++;
590	      const x2 = rng.int(lx, rx);
591	      const y2 = rng.int(ty, by);
592	      if (st.inBounds(x2, y2) && st.wall[st.idx(x2, y2)] === 87 && !st.flags[st.idx(x2, y2)]) {
593	        // ±70 内已有 240，或 ±4 内有蜥蜴砖 226 → 拒绝（cs:34654-34675）
594	        let flag = false;
595	        for (let dx = -70; dx <= 70 && !flag; dx++) {
596	          for (let dy = -70; dy <= 70; dy++) {
597	            const xx = x2 + dx, yy = y2 + dy;
598	            if (!st.inBounds(xx, yy)) continue;
599	            const k = st.idx(xx, yy);
600	            if (!st.flags[k]) continue;
601	            const sh = TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]]?.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
602	            if (sh === 240) { flag = true; break; }
603	            if (dx >= -4 && dx <= 4 && dy >= -4 && dy <= 4 && sh === 226) { flag = true; break; }
604	          }
605	        }
606	        if (!flag && placeWallHangingTemple(st, x2, y2, 88)) count--;
607	      }
608	      if (fails > 10000) break;
609	    }
610	  }
611	}

613	/** PlaceTile(105, style)（神庙雕像）：2×3，锚点=底行左列的下一格语义（与 StatuesPass 一致） */
614	function placeStatueTemple(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, style: number): boolean {
615	  const STATUE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_105_statue'];
616	  if (STATUE === undefined) return false;
617	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
618	    for (let dy = -2; dy <= 0; dy++) {
619	      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy) || st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)]) return false;
620	    }
621	  }
622	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
623	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) {
624	      // 雕像帧 55 列换行（cs:39716-39720；神庙 style 43-45 <55 本可不过防御性同款）
625	      const row = Math.floor(style / 55);
626	      st.setTileSilent(x + dx, y - 2 + dy, STATUE, (style - row * 55) * 36 + dx * 18, row * 54 + dy * 18);
627	    }
628	  }
629	  return true;
630	}
631	
632	/** PlaceTile(240, style)（神庙墙挂饰）：3×3，帧基 = (style%36)*54 / floor(style/36)*54 */
633	function placeWallHangingTemple(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, style: number): boolean {
634	  const HANGING = TILE_BY_KEY['v_240_wall_hangings_3x3'];
635	  if (HANGING === undefined) return false;
636	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {
637	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) {
638	      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy) || st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)]) return false;
639	    }
640	  }
641	  const bx = (style % 36) * 54;
642	  const bys = Math.floor(style / 36) * 54;
643	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) {
644	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) {
645	      st.setTileSilent(x + dx, y + dy, HANGING, bx + dx * 18, bys + dy * 18);
646	    }
647	  }
648	  return true;
649	}
650	
651	/** 玛雅陷阱(WorldGen.cs:2945-3235):压板 135(style6)→ 陷阱 137,路径铺三色随机导线 */
652	function mayanTrap(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, x2: number, y2: number): boolean {
653	  const PLATE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_135_pressure_plates']!;
654	  const TRAP = TILE_BY_KEY['v_137_traps']!;
655	  const solid = (x: number, y: number) => st.inBounds(x, y) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] !== 0;
656	  const wireBit = (n: number) => n === 0 ? TileStore.WIRE_RED : n === 1 ? TileStore.WIRE_BLUE : TileStore.WIRE_GREEN;
657	  const setW = (x: number, y: number, n: number) => { st.wire[st.idx(x, y)] |= wireBit(n); };
658	  const getW = (x: number, y: number) => {
659	    const b = st.wire[st.idx(x, y)];
660	    if (b & TileStore.WIRE_RED) return 0;
661	    if (b & TileStore.WIRE_BLUE) return 1;
662	    if (b & TileStore.WIRE_GREEN) return 2;
663	    return -1;
664	  };
665	  const pickColor = (x: number, y: number) => {
666	    const ex = getW(x, y);
667	    return ex >= 0 ? ex : rng.int(0, 2);
668	  };
669	  // num1:1/3 概率横向(L2947-2949)
670	  const kind = rng.int(0, 2) === 0 ? 0 : 1;
671	  // 落点向下找实心(L2950-2962)
672	  const px = x2;
673	  let fy = y2;
674	  while (!solid(px, fy)) {
675	    fy++;
676	    if (fy >= st.h - 300) return false;
677	  }
678	  const sh = TILE_BY_KEY['v_226_lihzahrd_brick']!;
679	  if (st.type[st.idx(px, fy)] === sh || TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(px, fy)]]?.vanilla?.sheet === 10) return false;
680	  const j = fy - 1;
681	  if (st.liquid[st.idx(px, j)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(px, j)] === 2) return false;
682	  // 周围须净空(L2967)
683	  const nactive = (x: number, y: number) => st.inBounds(x, y) && st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] !== 0;
684	  if (nactive(px, j) || nactive(px - 1, j) || nactive(px + 1, j) || nactive(px, j - 1)
685	    || nactive(px - 1, j - 1) || nactive(px + 1, j - 1) || nactive(px, j - 2)
686	    || nactive(px - 1, j - 2) || nactive(px + 1, j - 2)) return false;
687	
688	  if (kind === 0) {
689	    // 横向:同高走廊两端墙装飞镖陷阱,压板居中(L2971-3119)
690	    const ty = j - rng.int(0, 2);
691	    let lx = px;
692	    while (!solid(lx, ty)) lx--;
693	    let rx = px;
694	    while (!solid(rx, ty)) rx++;
695	    let flagL = (px - lx) > 5 && (px - lx) < 50 && solid(lx, ty + 1);
696	    let flagR = (rx - px) > 5 && (rx - px) < 50 && solid(rx, ty + 1);
697	    if (!flagL && !flagR) return false;
698	    let dir: number, tx: number;
699	    if (flagL && flagR) {
700	      dir = 1; tx = lx;
701	      if (rng.int(0, 1) === 0) { tx = rx; dir = -1; }
702	    } else if (flagR) { tx = rx; dir = -1; }
703	    else { tx = lx; dir = 1; }
704	    if (st.type[st.idx(tx, ty)] === PLATE || st.type[st.idx(tx, ty)] === TRAP) return false;
705	    if (st.wall[st.idx(tx, ty)] !== 87) return false;
706	    // 压板 + 陷阱(L3013-3051)。style:近距(<10)2/3 概率超级飞镖,否则飞镖
707	    const style1 = Math.abs(tx - px) < 10 && rng.int(0, 2) !== 0 ? 2 : 1;
708	    st.setTileSilent(px, j, PLATE, 0, 6 * 18);
709	    st.setTileSilent(tx, ty, TRAP, dir === 1 ? 18 : 0, style1 * 18);
710	    // L 形铺线:压板 → 陷阱(L3068-3118,含两端点)
711	    const num5 = pickColor(px, j);
712	    let wx = px, wy = j;
713	    while (wx !== tx || wy !== ty) {
714	      setW(wx, wy, num5);
715	      if (wx > tx) wx--;
716	      else if (wx < tx) wx++;
717	      setW(wx, wy, num5);
718	      if (wy > ty) wy--;
719	      else if (wy < ty) wy++;
720	      setW(wx, wy, num5);
721	    }
722	    setW(tx, ty, num5);
723	    return true;
724	  }
725	  // 纵向:头顶找天花板,天花板装下向火焰陷阱,两侧墙追加同排陷阱(L3120-3235)
726	  let uy = j;
727	  while (!solid(px, uy)) {
728	    uy--;
729	    if (uy < gs.rockLevel) return false;
730	  }
731	  const dist = Math.abs(uy - j);
732	  if (dist < 3) return false;
733	  if (st.type[st.idx(px, uy)] === PLATE || st.type[st.idx(px, uy)] === TRAP
734	    || TILE_DEFS[st.type[st.idx(px, uy)]]?.vanilla?.sheet === 10 || st.wall[st.idx(px, uy)] !== 87) return false;
735	  const num9 = pickColor(px, j);
736	  const style2 = dist < 16 && rng.int(0, 2) !== 0 ? 4 : 3;
737	  st.setTileSilent(px, j, PLATE, 0, 6 * 18);
738	  st.setTileSilent(px, uy, TRAP, 0, style2 * 18);
739	  // 两侧墙追加陷阱(L3146-3178)
740	  for (let side = 0; side < 2; side++) {
741	    let steps = rng.int(1, 4);
742	    let ix = px;
743	    const d = side === 1 ? 1 : -1;
744	    while (steps > 0) {
745	      steps--;
746	      ix += d;
747	      if (solid(ix, uy - 1) && !solid(ix, uy + 1)) {
748	        st.setTileSilent(ix, uy, TRAP, d === 1 ? 18 : 0, style2 * 18);
749	        st.wire[st.idx(ix, uy)] |= wireBit(num9);
750	      } else break;
751	    }
752	  }
753	  // 竖向铺线:压板 → 天花板陷阱(L3179-3235)
754	  {
755	    let wx = px, wy = j;
756	    while (wx !== px || wy !== uy) {
757	      setW(wx, wy, num9);
758	      if (wy > uy) wy--;
759	      else if (wy < uy) wy++;
760	      setW(wx, wy, num9);
761	    }
762	    setW(px, uy, num9);
763	  }
764	  return true;
765	}

So it's a self-made repair pass compensating for our own engine gaps. Per the audit's categories, this is a "自制兜底/强制路径". But is it harmful? It removes fragments that vanilla wouldn't have (vanilla never creates fragments). It doesn't consume RNG (runMultitileFragmentSweepPass takes no rng) → no rng-stream impact. Verdict: acceptable (engine-difference compensation), BUT it masks real bugs and the sweep list includes things like crystal_heart which vanilla... hmm. It's a repair pass. I'd report as acceptable with a note: it's an invention with no vanilla counterpart; it consumes no RNG; it only removes artifacts of our own incomplete placement. // 阶段 4 结构 pass:
// - FloatingIslands(pass 19,L5552 + CloudIsland L47397 简化):云岛(云 189 壳 + 土芯 + 草皮)
// - LifeCrystals(pass 55,L7828):area×2e-5 个生命水晶(岩层空腔地上)
// - HellHouses 已由 HellFortPass.runHellFortPass 取代
// - SurfaceDecor:生物群系感知地表(丛林/雪原树密度、沙漠仙人掌、丛林藤蔓/蘑菇)
import type { TileStore } from '../../TileStore';
import type { RNG } from '../../../core/rng';
import type { GenState } from './GenState';
import { T, TILE_BY_KEY, TILE_DEFS } from '../../../data/tiles';
import { ITEM_BY_KEY } from '../../../data/items';
import { placeBuriedChest } from './BuriedChestsPass';
import { waterBoxPalmCheck, growPalmTree} from './TreePass';
import { idsByVanillaSheet } from './TileRunner';
import { solidTile as _solidTileHB } from './HalfBrickPass';

const CLOUD = TILE_BY_KEY['v_189_cloud_block']!;
/** 雨云 196：CloudIsland/CloudLake 的雨云凸包（1/4）与岛上方 puff（1/2）专用；
 *  岛盘主体是云 189（cs:78724 num8）——196 误作主体会令土芯 type==189 判定恒假。
 *  云下雨幕由 WaterfallRenderer 画（WaterfallManager :204 type 11） */
const RAIN_CLOUD = TILE_BY_KEY['v_196_rain_cloud_block']!;
const EBONSAND = TILE_BY_KEY['v_112_ebonsand_block']!;
const CRIMSAND = TILE_BY_KEY['v_234_crimsand_block']!;
const JUNGLE_GRASS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_60_jungle_grass_block']!;
const CACTUS = TILE_BY_KEY['v_80_cactus']!;
const VINE = TILE_BY_KEY['v_52_vines']!;
const HEART = TILE_BY_KEY['crystal_heart']!;


// ================= Pass 23 FloatingIslands（cs:12976-13171 + CloudIsland cs:78618 + CloudLake cs:79068）1:1 =================
// 注册序 num=(int)(w*0.0008) 座岛 + skyLakes(4200 宽=1) 座天湖；
// 采样：x=Next(0.1w,0.9w) 避中央 ±150 → 与已放岛距 ≥180 → 该列自 y=200 起找首个活性格
// → y=Next(90, surf-100) 再 min(y, GenVars.worldSurfaceLow-50)；预算 w 次逐岛。
// 距离用 sqrt(dx²+dy²)（原版 Math.Sqrt——Math.hypot 有 ulp 级差异，禁用）
export function runFloatingIslandsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
  const { w, h } = st;
  const isCount = Math.trunc(w * 0.0008);          // 3（4200 宽）
  const skyLakes = 1;                               // GenVars.skyLakes（w>8000/6000 才 +1/+1）
  const total = isCount + skyLakes;
  const housesX: number[] = [];
  const housesY: number[] = [];
  let lakes = 0;
  for (let n = 0; n < total; n++) {
    let budget = w;
    while (--budget > 0) {
      let ok = true;
      let x = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.1), Math.floor(w * 0.9) - 1);   // Next(0.1w, 0.9w)
      while (x > w / 2 - 150 && x < w / 2 + 150) x = rng.int(Math.floor(w * 0.1), Math.floor(w * 0.9) - 1);
      for (let j = 0; j < housesX.length; j++) {
        if (x > housesX[j]! - 180 && x < housesX[j]! + 180) { ok = false; break; }
      }
      if (!ok) continue;
      ok = false;
      let surf = 0;
      for (let y = 200; y < gs.worldSurface && y < h; y++) {
        if (st.flags[st.idx(x, y)]) { surf = y; ok = true; break; }
      }
      if (!ok) continue;
      budget = -1;
      // GenVars.worldSurfaceLow（raw 截断）＝ gs.worldSurfaceLow（正数 floor≡trunc）
      const y = Math.min(rng.int(90, surf - 101), gs.worldSurfaceLow - 50);
      // GenVars.worldSurfaceLow（raw double 截断）——注意非 gs.worldSurfaceLow
      const isLake = lakes >= isCount;
          if (isLake) cloudLake(st, rng, x, y);
      else cloudIsland(st, rng, x, y);
          housesX.push(x); housesY.push(y);
      gs.floatingIslands.push({ x, y, style: 0, isLake });
      if (!isLake) lakes++;
    }
  }
}

/** WillWaterPlacedHereStayPut（cs:79050）：下/左/右三邻 = (活性实心非平台) 或 满水 */
function waterStaysPut(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  const side = (ax: number, ay: number): boolean => {
    if (!st.inBounds(ax, ay)) return false;
    const k = st.idx(ax, ay);
    return (st.flags[k] !== 0 && !!TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]]?.solid && !TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]]?.platform)
      || st.liquid[k] === 255;
  };
  return side(x, y + 1) && side(x - 1, y) && side(x + 1, y);
}

/** CloudIsland（cs:78618-79066）1:1：①云盘(189) → ②底缘雨云凸包 → ③土芯(云→土) →
 *  ④土面云斑 → ⑤墙 73 → ⑥岛面水池 → ⑦岛上方云 puff + 中心注水 */
function cloudIsland(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, ci: number, cj: number): void {

  let vy = rng.int(-20, -11) * 0.02;

    const y0 = Math.max(0, Math.trunc(py - num * 0.5)), y1 = Math.min(st.h, Math.trunc(py + num * 0.5));
    num2 = num * rng.int(80, 119) * 0.01;

        if (y <= top) continue;
        const ddx = Math.abs(x - px), ddy = Math.abs(y - py) * 3;

109	function createRooms(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, ox: number, oy: number): Rect[] {
110	  let py = oy;
111	  for (let i = 0; i < 200 && !solidAt(st, ox, py); i++) py++;
112	  if (!solidAt(st, ox, py) || py === oy) return [];
113	  const main = findRoom(st, ox, py);
114	  const above = findRoom(st, main.x + (main.w >> 1), main.y + 1);
115	  const below = findRoom(st, main.x + (main.w >> 1), main.y + main.h + 10);
116	  below.y = main.y + main.h - 1;
117	  const pctAbove = solidPct(st, above);
118	  const pctBelow = solidPct(st, below);
119	  main.y += 3; above.y += 3; below.y += 3;
120	  const rooms: Rect[] = [];
121	  if (rng.next() > pctAbove + 0.2) rooms.push(above);
122	  rooms.push(main);
123	  if (rng.next() > pctBelow + 0.2) rooms.push(below);
124	  rooms.sort((a, b) => a.y - b.y);
125	  return rooms;
126	}
127	
128	function getHouseType(st: TileStore, rooms: Rect[]): Theme {
129	  const count = (t: number) => {
130	    let n = 0;
131	    for (const r of rooms) {
132	      for (let x = r.x - 10; x < r.x + r.w + 10; x++) {
133	        for (let y = r.y - 10; y < r.y + r.h + 10; y++) {
134	          if (activeAt(st, x, y) && st.type[st.idx(x, y)] === t) n++;
135	        }
136	      }
137	    }
138	    return n;
139	  };
140	  const scores: Array<[Theme, number]> = [
141	    ['wood', count(SC0) + count(SC1)],
142	    ['jungle', count(SC59) + count(JUNGLE60) * 10],
143	    ['mushroom', count(SC59) + count(MUSH70) * 10],
144	    ['ice', count(SC147) + count(SC161)],
145	    ['desert', count(SC397) + count(SC396) + count(SC53)],
146	    ['granite', count(SC368)],
147	    ['marble', count(SC367)],
148	  ];
149	  let best: [Theme, number] = scores[0];
150	  for (let i = 1; i < scores.length; i++) if (best[1] < scores[i][1]) best = scores[i];
151	  return best[0];
152	}

154	// ---------- 家具/陈设放置 ----------
155	/** 底锚(bottom)/顶锚(top)放置 framed 物件;帧 = style*fw*18 步长 */
156	function placeFurniture(st: TileStore, key: string, x: number, y: number, style = 0, anchor: 'bottom' | 'top' = 'bottom'): boolean {
157	  const t = TILE_BY_KEY[key];
158	  if (t === undefined) return false;
159	  const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
160	  const fw = d.vanilla?.fw ?? 1;
161	  const w = d.w ?? fw, h = d.h ?? (d.vanilla?.fh ?? 1);
162	  const topY = anchor === 'bottom' ? y - (h - 1) : y;
163	  for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
164	    for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
165	      if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, topY + dy) || st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, topY + dy)]) return false;
166	    }
167	  }
168	  // 样式基偏移:权威布局表(FurnitureStyle.ts,竖排/换行族与通用横排统一)
169	  const sheet = d.vanilla?.sheet ?? -1;
170	  const [bx, by] = furnitureStyleBase(sheet, style, fw);
171	  for (let dx = 0; dx < w; dx++) {
172	    for (let dy = 0; dy < h; dy++) {
173	      st.setTileSilent(x + dx, topY + dy, t, bx + dx * 18, by + dy * 18);
174	    }
175	  }
176	  return true;
177	}
178	
179	/** 雕像(2×3,style*36 步长);STATUES_WITH_TRAPS 命中 → 邻近飞镖陷阱+接线 */
180	function placeStatue(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number, idx: number): boolean {
181	  const [sheet, style] = STATUE_LIST[idx];
182	  const tile = sheet === 349 ? STATUE_349 : STATUE_105;
183	  if (!tile || !st.inBounds(x + 1, y - 2)) return false;
184	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
185	    for (let dy = -2; dy <= 0; dy++) {
186	      if (st.flags[st.idx(x + dx, y + dy)]) return false;
187	    }
188	  }
189	  // 雕像帧换行（Place2xX case 105，cs:39716-39720）：每行 55 列——
190	  // frameX=(style%55)*36、frameY=floor(style/55)*54（与 StatuesPass 同实现；
191	  // 此前单轴 style*36，style≥55 越界错位）
192	  const row = Math.floor(style / 55);
193	  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
194	    for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) {
195	      st.setTileSilent(x + dx, y - 2 + dy, tile, (style - row * 55) * 36 + dx * 18, row * 54 + dy * 18);
196	    }
197	  }
198	  if (STATUES_WITH_TRAPS.has(idx)) placeStatueTrap(st, x, y);
199	  return true;
200	}

202	// ---------- 画(RandHousePicture cs:35336 / Desert cs:35286) ----------
203	const PIC_240 = [26, 28, 20, 21, 22, 24, 25, 33, 34, 35, 76, 77, 78, 79, 82, 96, 98, 100];
204	const PIC_245 = [0, 3, 5, 6, 14, -1, 19, 20, 21]; // -1 → 15+rng(4)
205	const PIC_246 = [1, 30, 31, 32, 33, 34, 35, 36];
206	function randHousePicture(rng: RNG): [string, number] {
207	  let n = rng.int(0, 3);
208	  if (n >= 3 && rng.int(0, 1) !== 0) n = rng.int(0, 2);
209	  if (n <= 1) return ['v_240_wall_hangings_3x3', PIC_240[rng.int(0, 17)]];
210	  if (n === 2) {
211	    let s = rng.int(0, 8);
212	    if (s === 0 && rng.int(0, 2) !== 0) s = rng.int(0, 8);
213	    const style = s === 5 ? 15 + rng.int(0, 3) : PIC_245[s];
214	    return ['v_245_paintings_2x3', style];
215	  }
216	  return ['v_246_paintings_3x2', PIC_246[rng.int(0, 7)]];
217	}
218	function randHousePictureDesert(rng: RNG): [string, number] {
219	  const n = rng.int(0, 3);
220	  if (n <= 1) return ['v_240_wall_hangings_3x3', [63, 64, 65, 66, 67, 68][rng.int(0, 5)]];
221	  if (n === 2) return ['v_245_paintings_2x3', 7 + rng.int(0, 1)];
222	  return ['v_242_paintings_6x4', 37 + rng.int(0, 5)];
223	}
224	
225	// ---------- Blotches 相干噪声(Modifiers.Blotches 近似:2 格团簇值噪声) ----------
226	function makeBlotch(seed: number): (x: number, y: number) => number {
227	  const hash = (x: number, y: number) => {
228	    let h = (x * 374761393 + y * 668265263 + seed * 1442695041) | 0;
229	    h = Math.imul(h ^ (h >>> 13), 1274126177);
230	    return ((h ^ (h >>> 16)) >>> 0) / 4294967295;
231	  };
232	  return (x: number, y: number) => {
233	    const gx = Math.floor(x / 2), gy = Math.floor(y / 2);
234	    const fx = x / 2 - gx, fy = y / 2 - gy;
235	    const sx = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx), sy = fy * fy * (3 - 2 * fy);
236	    const a = hash(gx, gy), b = hash(gx + 1, gy), c = hash(gx, gy + 1), d = hash(gx + 1, gy + 1);
237	    return a + (b - a) * sx + (c - a) * sy + (a - b - c + d) * sx * sy;
238	  };
239	}
240	
241	// ---------- AgeRoom 七主题蚀变 ----------
242	/** PlaceTight 冰系钟乳石(PlaceUncheckedStalactite 冰分支:上方∈{147,161,163,164,200}) */
243	function placeStalactiteIce(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): void {
244	  if (!solidAt(st, x, y - 1) || activeAt(st, x, y) || activeAt(st, x, y + 1)) return;
245	  const above = st.type[st.idx(x, y - 1)];
246	  if (above !== SNOW147 && above !== ICE161
247	    && above !== TILE_BY_KEY['v_163_corrupt_ice_block']! && above !== sheetTile(164)
248	    && above !== TILE_BY_KEY['v_200_crimson_ice_block']!) return;
249	  const preferSmall = rng.int(0, 1) === 0;
250	  const variation = rng.int(0, 2);
251	  // 冰族专属列（GetDesiredStalagtiteStyle :38800-38808 重定型）：147/161→0、
252	  // 163→594、164→540、200→648——曾全写 fx0 冰柱列（163/164/200 错列）
253	  const base = above === SNOW147 || above === ICE161 ? 0
254	    : above === TILE_BY_KEY['v_163_corrupt_ice_block']! ? 594
255	    : above === sheetTile(164) ? 540 : 648;
256	  if (preferSmall) {
257	    st.setTileSilent(x, y, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 72);
258	  } else {
259	    st.setTileSilent(x, y, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 0);
260	    st.setTileSilent(x, y + 1, STALACTITE, base + variation * 18, 18);
261	  }
262	}
263	/** 花岗岩/大理石/沙漠钟乳石:原版 PlaceUncheckedStalactite 的冰/石双名单不含
264	 *  368/367/397 → 恒 no-op(原版语义,cs:38378 名单外静默返回) */
265	function placeStalactiteNoop(): void { /* 原版名单外无操作 */ }
266	
267	function ageRoom(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, r: Rect, theme: Theme, def: ThemeDef, seed: number): void {
268	  const blob = makeBlotch(seed);
269	  const wallGate = (x: number, y: number, dither: number, blobP: number) =>
270	    rng.next() < dither && blob(x, y) < blobP;
271	  const PAINTINGS = new Set([sheetTile(240), sheetTile(241), sheetTile(242),
272	    TILE_BY_KEY['v_245_paintings_2x3']!, TILE_BY_KEY['v_246_paintings_3x2']!]);
273	
274	  if (theme === 'wood') {
275	    // 蛛网(WoodHouseBuilder.AgeRoom):W*H/16 次 2×2 斑点;Blotches(2,2) 恒过
276	    const count = Math.floor(r.w * r.h / 16);
277	    for (let c = 0; c < count; c++) {
278	      const x = rng.int(1, r.w - 2) + r.x;
279	      const y = rng.int(1, r.h - 2) + r.y;
280	      for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {
281	        for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {
282	          if (activeAt(st, x + dx, y + dy)) continue;
283	          if (rng.next() < 0.5) st.setTileSilent(x + dx, y + dy, COBWEB, 0, 0);
284	        }
285	      }
286	    }
287	    // 墙侵蚀:OnlyWalls(27);地下清墙/地表换土墙 2
288	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
289	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
290	        if (st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.wall) continue;
291	        if (PAINTINGS.has(st.type[st.idx(x, y)])) continue;
292	        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) {
293	          st.setWallSilent(x, y, y > gs.worldSurface ? 0 : 2);
294	        }
295	      }
296	    }
297	    return;
298	  }
299	  if (theme === 'ice') {
300	    // 321 →(0.6+团簇)→ 161 →(0.8)→ 147
301	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
302	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
303	        if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;
304	        if (rng.next() < 0.6 && blob(x, y) < 0.6) {
305	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, ICE161);
306	          if (rng.next() < 0.8) st.setTileSilent(x, y, SNOW147);
307	        }
308	      }
309	    }
310	    // 161 行垂冰钟乳石(顶行向内/底行向下)
311	    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {
312	      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {
313	        if (activeAt(st, x, rowY) && st.type[st.idx(x, rowY)] === ICE161 && rng.next() < 0.5) {
314	          placeStalactiteIce(st, rng, x, rowY + 1);
315	        }
316	      }
317	    }
318	    // 墙侵蚀(无 OnlyWalls):地下清墙/地表换墙 40;跳过画
319	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
320	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
321	        if (PAINTINGS.has(st.type[st.idx(x, y)])) continue;
322	        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.8) {
323	          st.setWallSilent(x, y, y > gs.worldSurface ? 0 : 40);
324	        }
325	      }
326	    }
327	    return;
328	  }
329	  if (theme === 'jungle') {
330	    // 158 →(0.6+团簇)→ 60 →(0.8)→ 59
331	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
332	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
333	        if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;
334	        if (rng.next() < 0.6 && blob(x, y) < 0.6) {
335	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, JUNGLE60);
336	          if (rng.next() < 0.8) st.setTileSilent(x, y, MUD59);
337	        }
338	      }
339	    }
340	    // 60 行垂藤(ActionVines(3, room.Height, 62):长 3..H,遇实心止)
341	    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {
342	      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {
343	        if (activeAt(st, x, rowY) && st.type[st.idx(x, rowY)] === JUNGLE60 && rng.next() < 0.5) {
344	          const len = rng.int(3, r.h);
345	          for (let i = 0; i < len && !activeAt(st, x, rowY + 1 + i); i++) {
346	            st.setTileSilent(x, rowY + 1 + i, VINE62, 0, 0);
347	          }
348	        }
349	      }
350	    }
351	    // 墙 → 64(无 OnlyWalls)
352	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
353	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
354	        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, 64);
355	      }
356	    }
357	    return;
358	  }
359	  if (theme === 'mushroom') {
360	    // 190 →(0.7+团簇0.5)→ 70
361	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
362	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
363	        if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;
364	        if (rng.next() < 0.7 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setTileSilent(x, y, MUSH70);
365	      }
366	    }
367	    // 70 行上方发光菇 71(Offset(0,-1),IsEmpty)
368	    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {
369	      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {
370	        if (activeAt(st, x, rowY) && st.type[st.idx(x, rowY)] === MUSH70 && rng.next() < 0.6) {
371	          if (!activeAt(st, x, rowY - 1)) st.setTileSilent(x, rowY - 1, SHROOM71, 0, 0);
372	        }
373	      }
374	    }
375	    // 墙清空(无 OnlyWalls)
376	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
377	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
378	        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, 0);
379	      }
380	    }
381	    return;
382	  }
383	  if (theme === 'granite' || theme === 'marble') {
384	    // 369→368 / 357→367(0.6+团簇);钟乳石 = 原版名单外 no-op;墙 → 180/178
385	    const to = theme === 'granite' ? GRANITE368 : MARBLE367;
386	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
387	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
388	        if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;
389	        if (rng.next() < 0.6 && blob(x, y) < 0.6) st.setTileSilent(x, y, to);
390	      }
391	    }
392	    for (const rowY of [r.y, r.y + r.h - 1]) {
393	      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {
394	        const t = st.type[st.idx(x, rowY)];
395	        if (activeAt(st, x, rowY) && t === to && rng.next() < 0.8) placeStalactiteNoop();
396	      }
397	    }
398	    for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
399	      for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
400	        if (rng.next() < 0.85 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, theme === 'granite' ? 180 : 178);
401	      }
402	    }
403	    return;
404	  }
405	  // desert:396 →(0.8+团簇0.2)→ 397;钟乳石名单外 no-op;OnlyWalls(187) → 墙 216
406	  for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
407	    for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
408	      if (st.type[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.tile || !activeAt(st, x, y)) continue;
409	      if (rng.next() < 0.8 && blob(x, y) < 0.2 && rng.next() < 0.5) st.setTileSilent(x, y, HARDSAND397);
410	    }
411	  }
412	  for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
413	    for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
414	      if (st.wall[st.idx(x, y)] !== def.wall) continue;
415	      if (rng.next() < 0.8 && blob(x, y) < 0.5) st.setWallSilent(x, y, 216);
416	    }
417	  }
418	}
419	
420	// ---------- FillRooms(画/烛台交替 + 装饰循环) ----------
421	function fillRooms(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, r: Rect, def: ThemeDef, theme: Theme): void {
422	  // 家具七选(HouseBuilder.FillRooms choices;沙漠 Tables2 → tile 469)
423	  const choices: Array<[string, number]> = [
424	    [def.tables2 ? 'v_469_tables_group_2' : 'table', def.table],
425	    ['anvil', 0],
426	    ['workbench', def.workbench],
427	    ['v_86_loom', 0],
428	    ['piano', def.piano],
429	    ['keg', 0],
430	    ['bookcase', def.bookcase],
431	  ];
432	  // 画/烛台交替:switch(i + parity%2) 仅 0/1 两档有内容(i≥2 落空,原版同款)
433	  const n = Math.floor(r.w / 8);
434	  const step = Math.floor(r.w / (n + 1));
435	  const parity = rng.int(0, 1);
436	  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
437	    const x = (i + 1) * step + r.x;
438	    if ((i + (parity % 2)) % 2 === 0) {
439	      // 画:Y + min(H/2, H-5)
440	      const y = r.y + Math.min(Math.floor(r.h / 2), r.h - 5);
441	      const [key, style] = theme === 'desert' ? randHousePictureDesert(rng) : randHousePicture(rng);
442	      placeFurniture(st, key, x, y, style);
443	    } else {
444	      // 烛台:顶行悬挂(原版 +54 点亮帧——本引擎烛台恒点亮,帧差不建模)
445	      const y = r.y + 1;
446	      placeFurniture(st, 'candelabra', x, y, rng.int(0, 5), 'top');
447	    }
448	  }
449	  // 装饰循环:W/8+3 件
450	  const decoCount = Math.floor(r.w / 8) + 3;
451	  for (let k = 0; k < decoCount; k++) {
452	    const x = rng.int(0, r.w - 4) + 1 + r.x;
453	    const y = r.y + r.h - 2;
454	    switch (rng.int(0, 3)) {
455	      case 0: // 小堆 185(PlaceSmallPile size1:style*18)
456	        if (!activeAt(st, x, y)) st.setTileSilent(x, y, SMALL_PILE, rng.int(31, 33) * 18, 0);
457	        break;
458	      case 1: // 骨堆 186
459	        placeFurniture(st, 'v_186_decos_3x2', x, y, rng.int(22, 25));
460	        break;
461	      case 2: // 雕像(statueList[Next(2, len)],陷阱雕像照接)
462	        placeStatue(st, x, y, rng.int(2, STATUE_LIST.length - 1));
463	        break;
464	      case 3: {
465	        const [key, style] = choices[rng.int(0, choices.length - 1)];
466	        placeFurniture(st, key, x, y, style);
467	        break;
468	      }
469	    }
470	  }
471	}
472	
473	// ---------- 楼梯 / 门 / 平台 / 梁 ----------
474	/** CreateStairsList + PlaceStairs:相邻房间对角平台链 + 顶步 4×1 平台 + 斜坡 */
475	function placeStairs(st: TileStore, rooms: Rect[], def: ThemeDef): void {
476	  for (let i = 1; i < rooms.length; i++) {
477	    const cur = rooms[i], prev = rooms[i - 1];
478	    const leftGap = prev.x - cur.x;
479	    const rightGap = cur.x + cur.w - (prev.x + prev.w);
480	    let from: { x: number; y: number };
481	    if (leftGap > rightGap) from = { x: cur.x + cur.w - 1, y: cur.y + 1 };
482	    else from = { x: cur.x, y: cur.y + 1 };
483	    const steps = (cur.y + cur.h - 1) - from.y;
484	    const dir = leftGap > rightGap ? -1 : 1;
485	    for (let s = 0; s < steps; s++) {
486	      const sx = from.x + dir * (s + 1), sy = from.y + s;
487	      if (!st.inBounds(sx, sy)) continue;
488	      st.setTileSilent(sx, sy, PLATFORM, 0, 0);
489	      st.slope[st.idx(sx, sy)] = dir === 1 ? 1 : 2;
490	    }
491	    // 顶步上方 4×1 平台(清空+补墙+平台)
492	    const px = from.x + (dir === 1 ? 1 : -4);
493	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 4; dx++) {
494	      if (!st.inBounds(px + dx, from.y - 1)) continue;
495	      st.setTileSilent(px + dx, from.y - 1, PLATFORM, 0, 0);
496	      st.setWallSilent(px + dx, from.y - 1, def.wall);
497	    }
498	  }
499	}
500	
501	/** FindSideExit(Up 搜索 + AreaOr(4,3) 任一非实心) */
502	function findSideExit(st: TileStore, wallX: number, wallY: number, wallH: number, isLeft: boolean): number | null {
503	  const startX = wallX + (isLeft ? -4 : 0);
504	  for (let k = 0; k < wallH - 3; k++) {
505	    const y = wallY + wallH - 3 - k;
506	    let any = false;
507	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 4 && !any; dx++) {
508	      for (let dy = 0; dy < 3 && !any; dy++) {
509	        if (!solidAt(st, startX + dx, y + dy)) any = true;
510	      }
511	    }
512	    if (any) return y;
513	  }
514	  return null;
515	}
516	
517	function placeDoors(st: TileStore, rooms: Rect[], def: ThemeDef): void {
518	  for (const r of rooms) {
519	    // 墙列矩形 (X, Y+1, 1, H-2)
520	    // 门禁（原版 PlaceTile(x, exitY)→PlaceDoor(j+1)，WorldGen.cs:31946）：mount
521	    // （exitY-1 实心）+ 地板（exitY+3 实心）缺一即不放门只留门洞——HouseBuilder
522	    // 的 forced=true 仅绕过首道空格门，门分支的锚点条件仍生效
523	    const tryDoor = (x: number, exitY: number) => {
524	      for (let dy = 0; dy < 3; dy++) st.setTileSilent(x, exitY + dy, 0);
525	      if (solidAt(st, x, exitY - 1) && solidAt(st, x, exitY + 3)) {
526	        placeDoorClosed(st, x, exitY, def.door);
527	      }
528	    };
529	    const exitR = findSideExit(st, r.x + r.w - 1, r.y + 1, r.h - 2, false);
530	    if (exitR !== null) tryDoor(r.x + r.w - 1, exitR);
531	    const exitL = findSideExit(st, r.x, r.y + 1, r.h - 2, true);
532	    if (exitL !== null) tryDoor(r.x, exitL);
533	  }
534	}
535	
536	/** FindVerticalExit(Left 搜索 + AreaOr(3,5) 任一非实心) */
537	function findVerticalExit(st: TileStore, rectX: number, rectY: number, rectW: number, isUp: boolean): number | null {
538	  const startX = rectX + rectW - 3;
539	  const startY = rectY + (isUp ? -5 : 0);
540	  for (let k = 0; k < rectW - 3; k++) {
541	    const x = startX - k;
542	    let any = false;
543	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 3 && !any; dx++) {
544	      for (let dy = 0; dy < 5 && !any; dy++) {
545	        if (!solidAt(st, x + dx, startY + dy)) any = true;
546	      }
547	    }
548	    if (any) return x;
549	  }
550	  return null;
551	}
552	
553	function placePlatforms(st: TileStore, rooms: Rect[]): void {
554	  const top = rooms[0], bot = rooms[rooms.length - 1];
555	  // 顶层顶壁(isUp:候选自壁上 5 格起)
556	  const exitT = findVerticalExit(st, top.x + 2, top.y, top.w - 4, true);
557	  if (exitT !== null) {
558	    for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) st.setTileSilent(exitT + dx, top.y, PLATFORM, 0, 0);
559	  }
560	  if (rooms.length > 1) {
561	    const exitB = findVerticalExit(st, bot.x + 2, bot.y + bot.h - 1, bot.w - 4, false);
562	    if (exitB !== null) {
563	      for (let dx = 0; dx < 3; dx++) st.setTileSilent(exitB + dx, bot.y + bot.h - 1, PLATFORM, 0, 0);
564	    }
565	  }
566	}
567	
568	/** CreateSupportBeamList + PlaceSupportBeams:6→4 步长梁列,落下一房顶/实心 */
569	function placeSupportBeams(st: TileStore, rooms: Rect[], def: ThemeDef): void {
570	  const minX = Math.min(...rooms.map((r) => r.x));
571	  const maxX = Math.max(...rooms.map((r) => r.x + r.w)) - 1;
572	  let step = 6;
573	  while (step > 4 && (maxX - minX) % step !== 0) step--;
574	  for (let x = minX; x <= maxX; x += step) {
575	    for (let ri = 0; ri < rooms.length; ri++) {
576	      const r = rooms[ri];
577	      if (x < r.x || x >= r.x + r.w) continue;
578	      const bottom = r.y + r.h;
579	      let limit = 50;
580	      for (let rj = ri + 1; rj < rooms.length; rj++) {
581	        const r2 = rooms[rj];
582	        if (x >= r2.x && x < r2.x + r2.w) limit = Math.min(limit, r2.y - bottom);
583	      }
584	      if (limit <= 0) continue;
585	      let found = -1;
586	      if (limit < 50) found = bottom + limit; // 原版:num7<50 视为命中下一房顶
587	      else {
588	        for (let y = bottom; y < bottom + limit; y++) {
589	          if (solidAt(st, x, y)) { found = y; break; }
590	        }
591	      }
592	      if (found < 0) continue;
593	      const height = found - bottom;
594	      if (height > 1 && st.type[st.idx(x, bottom - 1)] !== PLATFORM) {
595	        for (let y = bottom; y < found; y++) st.setTileSilent(x, y, def.beam, 0, 0);
596	      }
597	    }
598	  }
599	}
600	
601	// ---------- 宝箱四级联(PlaceChests) ----------
602	function placeChests(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink, rooms: Rect[], def: ThemeDef, theme: Theme): void {
603	  const tryAt = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
604	    const underground = y > gs.worldSurface;
605	    // 冰屋：显式 style 11 直入冰箱门（cs:36107 第一析取）→ 主件池 + 1/20 提炼机 +
606	    // 1/50 鱼覆盖块自动生效（旧实现显式 loot 绕过覆盖——审计三.4 修复）
607	    return placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y, {
608	      style: underground ? def.chestStyle : 0,
609	      tile: underground && def.chestTile2 ? CHEST2 : undefined,
610	      flags: theme === 'ice' ? { ice: true } : (!underground ? { wooden: true } : {}),
611	    });
612	  };
613	  // 级联 1:各房底行随机 10 次
614	  for (const r of rooms) {
615	    const floorY = r.y + r.h - 1;
616	    let ok = false;
617	    for (let t = 0; t < 10 && !ok; t++) ok = tryAt(rng.int(2, r.w - 3) + r.x, floorY);
618	    if (ok) return;
619	    for (let x = r.x + 2; x <= r.x + r.w - 2 && !ok; x++) ok = tryAt(x, floorY);
620	    if (ok) return;
621	  }
622	  // 级联 2:各房顶行(Y-1)
623	  for (const r of rooms) {
624	    const ceilY = r.y - 1;
625	    let ok = false;
626	    for (let t = 0; t < 10 && !ok; t++) ok = tryAt(rng.int(2, r.w - 3) + r.x, ceilY);
627	    if (ok) return;
628	    for (let x = r.x + 2; x <= r.x + r.w - 2 && !ok; x++) ok = tryAt(x, ceilY);
629	    if (ok) return;
630	  }
631	  // 级联 3:房外 ±30 随机 1000 次
632	  for (let t = 0; t < 1000; t++) {
633	    if (tryAt(rng.int(rooms[0].x - 30, rooms[0].x + 29), rng.int(rooms[0].y - 30, rooms[0].y + 29))) return;
634	  }
635	}
636	
637	// ---------- pass 主体 ----------
638	/** 地下小屋 pass(UndergroundHousesAndBuriedChests 的 CaveHouse 段,cs:17153-17178
639	 *  + 沙漠追加段 cs:17193-17219) */
640	export function runCaveHousesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
641	  const { w, h } = st;
642	  const count = Math.round(rng.int(35, 40) * (w * h) / 5040000);
643	  // 第 4 个 WorldGenRange：AdditionalDesertHouseCount（2-2 × WorldArea，
644	  // Configuration.json）——GetRandom 恒消费一次 Next（cs:17088）
645	  const desertHouses = rng.int(2, 2);
646	  let bastCount = 0;        // GenVars.extraBastStatueCount(≤2)
647	  let extractCount = 0;     // HouseBuilderContext.ExtractinatorCount
648	  const extractMax = rng.int(2, 4);
649	  let sharpCount = 0;       // context.SharpenerCount
650	  const sharpMax = rng.int(2, 4);
651	
652	  /** 单座小屋放置（HouseBuilder.Place 原序），返回是否成功 */
653	  const placeHouse = (ox: number, oy: number, oceanGate: boolean): boolean => {
654	    if (oceanGate && (ox < gs.beachDistance || ox > w - gs.beachDistance)) return false; // oceanDepths 回避
655	    const rooms = createRooms(st, rng, ox, oy);
656	    if (!rooms.length) return false;
657	    // 位置合法(InWorld 10 边距 / 底部 < h-220)+ 房间互不重叠
658	    let ok = true;
659	    for (const r of rooms) {
660	      if (r.x < 10 || r.y < 10 || r.x + r.w > w - 10 || r.y + r.h > h - 220) ok = false;
661	    }
662	    for (let a = 0; a < rooms.length && ok; a++) {
663	      for (let b = a + 1; b < rooms.length && ok; b++) {
664	        const A = rooms[a], B = rooms[b];
665	        if (A.x < B.x + B.w && B.x < A.x + A.w && A.y < B.y + B.h && B.y < A.y + A.h) ok = false;
666	      }
667	    }
668	    if (!ok) return false;
669	    const theme = getHouseType(st, rooms);
670	    const def = THEMES[theme];
671	    if (def.tile < 0 || def.beam < 0) return false;
672	    // 岩浆回避(granite 主题豁免,HouseUtils.AreRoomsValid)
673	    if (theme !== 'granite') {
674	      for (const r of rooms) {
675	        for (let x = r.x - 2; x < r.x + r.w + 2; x++) {
676	          for (let y = r.y - 2; y < r.y + r.h + 2; y++) {
677	            if (st.inBounds(x, y) && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] > 0 && st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] === 2) ok = false;
678	          }
679	        }
680	      }
681	      if (!ok) return false;
682	    }
683	
684	    // ---- HouseBuilder.Place 原序 ----
685	    // 1) PlaceEmptyRooms:外壳 + 内腔墙
686	    for (const r of rooms) {
687	      for (let x = r.x; x < r.x + r.w; x++) {
688	        for (let y = r.y; y < r.y + r.h; y++) {
689	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, def.tile);
690	          st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;
691	          st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] = 0;
692	        }
693	      }
694	      for (let x = r.x + 1; x < r.x + r.w - 1; x++) {
695	        for (let y = r.y + 1; y < r.y + r.h - 1; y++) {
696	          st.setTileSilent(x, y, 0);
697	          st.setWallSilent(x, y, def.wall);
698	        }
699	      }
700	    }
701	    // 2-4) 楼梯 / 门 / 平台
702	    placeStairs(st, rooms, def);
703	    placeDoors(st, rooms, def);
704	    placePlatforms(st, rooms);
705	    // 5) 支撑梁
706	    placeSupportBeams(st, rooms, def);
707	    // 6) 沙漠 Bast 石碑(优先件,≤2)
708	    if (theme === 'desert' && bastCount < 2) {
709	      const r = rooms[0];
710	      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_506_bast_statue', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) bastCount++;
711	    }
712	    // 7) FillRooms:画/烛台 + 装饰(每房)
713	    for (const r of rooms) fillRooms(st, rng, r, def, theme);
714	    // 8) AgeRoom:七主题蚀变
715	    const seed = rng.int(0, 1 << 30);
716	    for (const r of rooms) ageRoom(st, rng, gs, r, theme, def, seed);
717	    // 9) 宝箱(ChestChance=1.0,四级联)
718	    placeChests(st, rng, gs, chests, rooms, def, theme);
719	    // 10) 主题专属件:丛林磨刀站 / 沙漠提炼机
720	    if (theme === 'jungle' && sharpCount < sharpMax) {
721	      const r = rooms[0];
722	      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_377_sharpening_station', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) sharpCount++;
723	    }
724	    if (theme === 'desert' && extractCount < extractMax) {
725	      const r = rooms[rooms.length - 1];
726	      if (placeFurniture(st, 'v_219_extractinator', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, Math.max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)) extractCount++;
727	    }
728	    return true;
729	  };
730	
731	  for (let n = 0; n < count; n++) {
732	    const ox = rng.int(80, w - 81);
733	    const oy = rng.int(Math.ceil(gs.worldSurfaceHigh + 20), h - 231);
734	    placeHouse(ox, oy, true);
735	  }
736	
737	  // ---- 沙漠追加小屋（cs:17193-17219）：UndergroundDesertHiveLocation 内追加
738	  //  AdditionalDesertHouseCount 座（RandomRectanglePoint），失败预算 10000 重试同下标
739	  const ud = gs.undergroundDesert;
740	  if (ud && ud.x1 > ud.x0 && ud.y1 > ud.y0) {
741	    let ry = ud.y0;
742	    let rh = ud.y1 - ud.y0;
743	    if (ry < gs.worldSurface + 26) {          // cs:17200-17205：上缘压到 worldSurface+26 下
744	      const cut = Math.floor(gs.worldSurface) + 26 - ry;
745	      ry += cut;
746	      rh -= cut;
747	    }
748	    if (rh > 0) {
749	      let budget = 10000;
750	      for (let l = 0; l < desertHouses;) {
751	        if (budget <= 0) break;
752	        const hx = rng.int(ud.x0, ud.x1);      // RandomRectanglePoint: Next(X, X+Width)
753	        const hy = rng.int(ry, ry + rh);
754	        if (!placeHouse(hx, hy, false)) { budget--; continue; }
755	        l++;
756	      }
757	    }
758	  }
759	}
760	

```

1	using System;

7	namespace Terraria.GameContent.Biomes.CaveHouse;
8	
9	public static class HouseUtils
10	{
11		private static readonly bool[] BlacklistedTiles = TileID.Sets.Factory.CreateBoolSet(true, 225, 41, 43, 44, 226, 203, 112, 25, 151, 21, 467);
12	
13		private static readonly bool[] BeelistedTiles = TileID.Sets.Factory.CreateBoolSet(true, 41, 43, 44, 226, 203, 112, 25, 151, 21, 467);
14	
15		public static HouseBuilder CreateBuilder(Point origin, StructureMap structures)
16		{
17			List<Rectangle> list = CreateRooms(origin);
18			if (list.Count == 0 || !AreRoomLocationsValid(list))
19			{
20				return HouseBuilder.Invalid;
21			}
22			HouseType houseType = GetHouseType(list);
23			if (!AreRoomsValid(list, structures, houseType))
24			{
25				return HouseBuilder.Invalid;
26			}
27			return houseType switch
28			{
29				HouseType.Wood => new WoodHouseBuilder(list), 
30				HouseType.Desert => new DesertHouseBuilder(list), 
31				HouseType.Granite => new GraniteHouseBuilder(list), 
32				HouseType.Ice => new IceHouseBuilder(list), 
33				HouseType.Jungle => new JungleHouseBuilder(list), 
34				HouseType.Marble => new MarbleHouseBuilder(list), 
35				HouseType.Mushroom => new MushroomHouseBuilder(list), 
36				_ => new WoodHouseBuilder(list), 
37			};
38		}
39	
40		public static int GetMaxPossibleRoomsInABigAbandonedHouse()
41		{
42			if (WorldGen.SecretSeed.errorWorld.Enabled)
43			{
44				return 30;
45			}
46			return 7;
47		}
48	
49		public static int GetRandomizedRoomCountInABigAbandonedHouse()
50		{
51			int num = 7;
52			if (WorldGen.SecretSeed.errorWorld.Enabled)
53			{
54				num = WorldGen.genRand.Next(7, 31);
55			}
56			return Math.Max(0, num - WorldGen.genRand.Next(4));
57		}

59		private static List<Rectangle> CreateRooms_BigAbandonedHouses(Point origin)
60		{
61			List<Rectangle> list = new List<Rectangle>();
62			if (!WorldUtils.Find(origin, Searches.Chain(new Searches.Down(200), new Conditions.IsSolid()), out var result) || result == origin)
63			{
64				return new List<Rectangle>();
65			}
66			Rectangle lastRoom = FindRoom(result);
67			int randomizedRoomCountInABigAbandonedHouse = GetRandomizedRoomCountInABigAbandonedHouse();
68			if (randomizedRoomCountInABigAbandonedHouse == 0)
69			{
70				return list;
71			}
72			for (int i = 0; i < randomizedRoomCountInABigAbandonedHouse; i++)
73			{
74				Rectangle rectangle = FindRoom_BigAbandonedHouses(new Point(lastRoom.Center.X, lastRoom.Y + 1), lastRoom);
75				list.Add(rectangle);
76				lastRoom = rectangle;
77			}
78			for (int j = 0; j < list.Count; j++)
79			{
80				Rectangle rectangle2 = list[j];
81				rectangle2.Y += 3;
82			}
83			return list;
84		}
85	
86		private static List<Rectangle> CreateRooms(Point origin)
87		{
88			if (WorldGen.SecretSeed.GenerateBiggerAbandonedHouses)
89			{
90				return CreateRooms_BigAbandonedHouses(origin);
91			}
92			if (!WorldUtils.Find(origin, Searches.Chain(new Searches.Down(200), new Conditions.IsSolid()), out var result) || result == origin)
93			{
94				return new List<Rectangle>();
95			}
96			Rectangle item = FindRoom(result);
97			Rectangle rectangle = FindRoom(new Point(item.Center.X, item.Y + 1));
98			Rectangle rectangle2 = FindRoom(new Point(item.Center.X, item.Y + item.Height + 10));
99			rectangle2.Y = item.Y + item.Height - 1;
100			double roomSolidPrecentage = GetRoomSolidPrecentage(rectangle);
101			double roomSolidPrecentage2 = GetRoomSolidPrecentage(rectangle2);
102			item.Y += 3;
103			rectangle.Y += 3;
104			rectangle2.Y += 3;
105			List<Rectangle> list = new List<Rectangle>();
106			if (WorldGen.genRand.NextDouble() > roomSolidPrecentage + 0.2)
107			{
108				list.Add(rectangle);
109			}
110			list.Add(item);

/** 各排除表按原版 vanilla tile id 构建（经 idsByVanillaSheet 转内部 id） */
const T = { // 排除表用的原版 id 集
  SWITCH: idsByVanillaSheet(136),
  STATUE: idsByVanillaSheet(137),
  /** Loop B 主排除（16661）：137 雕像/48 尖刺/232 木刺/191 活木/151 沙岩砖/274 地牢砖? /75/76 黑曜石狱石砖 */
  LOOP_B_SKIP: idsByVanillaSheet(137, 48, 232, 191, 151, 274, 75, 76),
  /** 空位补角分支排除（16600/16602/16621）：151/274（不做补角材料）与 190/48/232（旁格排除） */
  NO_FILL: idsByVanillaSheet(151, 274),

  PREVENTS_SLOPES: idsByVanillaSheet(48, 137, 232, 191, 151, 274, 135, 442, 428),
};

/** blockType()==0 判定（悬顶分支旁格检查用）：非活性格或整砖格（half/slope 均为 0） */
function fullOrEmpty(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return true;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  return !st.flags[i] || (!st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0);
}

/** WorldGen.SolidTile(i, j, false) 等价（WorldGen.cs:42370）：

export function solidTile(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
  if (x < 0 || y < 0 || x >= st.w || y >= st.h) return false;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  // 原版 SolidTile 检查 active()（=flags）：TileRunner 幽灵 tile（有 type 无 flags）
  // 不得当实心——曾致补角分支实体化天空幽灵块成浮空半砖（2583 个）
  if (!st.flags[i]) return false;
  const t = st.type[i];
  if (t === 0) return false;
  const d = TILE_DEFS[t];
  return !!d && d.solid && !d.platform && !st.half[i] && st.slope[i] === 0;
}

HouseBuilder comparison — key deviations found:

CB1. **PlaceBiomeSpecificPriorityTool (Bast statue, desert)**: vanilla has a full 4-level cascade (random 10 tries at floor row per room, then scan floor row, then random 10 at ceiling row, then scan ceiling row) and only counts if placed. Ours: ONE attempt: `placeFurniture(st, 'v_506_bast_statue', r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, max(1, r.w - 3)), r.y + r.h - 2)` in rooms[0] only. Self-made single-shot with an invented x-range formula (`r.x + 1 + rng.int(0, max(1, r.w-3))` vs vanilla `Next(2, room.Width-2) + room.X`). Deviations: (a) single attempt instead of 4-level cascade; (b) invented x formula (off-by-one-ish: vanilla Next(2, W-2)+X ∈ [X+2, X+W-3]; ours [X+1, X+1+max(1,W-3)]); (c) only rooms[0] vs all rooms. Impact: geometry (fewer bast statues) + rng-stream (1 draw vs up to 10+ per level). Must-fix.

CB2. **PlaceBiomeSpecificTool (jungle sharpener / desert extractinator)**: same 4-level cascade in vanilla; ours single attempt in rooms[0] (sharpener) / rooms[last] (extractinator) with the invented x formula. Also: vanilla draws `_random.Next(2, 5)` FRESH for each house when comparing counts (`context.SharpenerCount < _random.Next(2,5)`) — ours pre-draws `sharpMax = rng.int(2, 4)` once per pass and reuses. MAJOR FINDING: vanilla `Modifiers.Blotches.Apply` is NOT coherent noise at all — it's:
1. `GenBase._random.NextDouble()` — one throwaway roll
2. `if (NextDouble() < _chance)` — a second roll to decide blotch
3. If blotch: 4 more rolls (Next(1-minX,1), Next(0,maxX), Next(1-minY,1), Next(0,maxY)) then applies the unit action to a random RECTANGLE [x+num, x+num2] × [y+num3, y+num4] — i.e., it applies the subsequent actions to a random blotch rectangle AROUND the point, and returns !flag (fails if any unit apply failed...). If not blotch: applies to the single point.

So vanilla Blotches: consumes 2 rolls always (+4 more when blotching), and the "blotch" spreads the action over a random rectangle, not a per-point probability gate.

Our implementation: a deterministic hash-based coherent noise (`makeBlotch`) used as `blob(x,y) < blobP` — a per-point gate with NO RNG consumption and completely different spatial statistics (vanilla: rectangular blotches that also APPLY to neighboring cells; ours: per-cell smooth noise threshold).

Impact:
- rng-stream: vanilla consumes 2 rolls per tile (+4 when blotch triggers) in EVERY AgeRoom Gen call that uses Blotches; ours consumes ZERO for the blotch part. This is a massive per-pass stream deviation (thousands of draws per house).
- geometry: vanilla's aging spreads via random rectangles (eroding walls/tiles in patches); ours thresholds per-cell with smooth noise → visually different erosion patterns.

Confirmed in both versions: `Dither(failureChance)` — the chain CONTINUES when `NextDouble() >= failureChance`. So Dither(0.85) passes only 15% of the time. The parameter is a FAILURE chance.

So all our `rng.next() < 0.85` gates are inverted: vanilla passes 15%, ours passes 85%. This is a MASSIVE geometry deviation across all seven AgeRoom themes (wall erosion, tile conversion, cobwebs use Dither() default 0.5 → 50% either way, no inversion there... wait: default 0.5 → pass when roll >= 0.5 → 50%. Ours `rng.next() < 0.5` → 50%. Symmetric, so the default case is unaffected. But all the 0.85/0.8/0.7/0.6 cases are inverted!).

Let me tabulate:
- Wood wall aging: vanilla Dither(0.85) → 15% of walls cleared; ours 85% cleared.
- Wood tile clearing: Dither(0.95) → 5% (also missing entirely from ours).
- Ice: Dither(0.6) → 40% convert to 161 (ours 60%); chained Dither(0.8) → 20% of those to 147 (ours 80%).
- Ice wall: Dither(0.85) → 15% (ours 85%).
- Jungle: 0.6 → 40% (ours 60%); 0.8 → 20% (ours 80%); wall 0.85 → 15% (ours 85%).
- Mushroom: 0.7 → 30% (ours 70%); shroom Dither(0.6) → 40% (ours 60%); wall 0.85 → 15% (ours 85%).
- Granite/Marble: 0.6 → 40% (ours 60%); stalactite dither 0.8 → 20% (ours 80%) [no-op anyway]; wall 0.85 → 15% (ours 85%).
- Desert: 0.8 → 20% (ours 80%); wall Dither(0.8) → 20% (ours 80%).
- Cobweb 2x2 inner Dither() → 50% ✓ same.
- Candelabra/painting: no dither.
- Ice/jungle stalactite Dither() → 50% ✓ same.
- Desert stalactite loop missing (rng-stream).

Also note: vanilla Dither rolls happen for EVERY tile in the shape (before OnlyTiles/OnlyWalls filters? NO — the chain order matters: e.g. `Dither(0.85), Blotches(), OnlyWalls(...)` — Dither is FIRST, so it rolls for every tile in the rect regardless of wall type. In ours: `if (st.wall !== def.wall) continue; if (rng.next() < 0.85 && ...)` — we check the wall FIRST and skip the roll! That's another stream deviation: vanilla consumes a roll for every tile in the room rect; ours only for tiles matching the wall. HUGE rng-stream difference (and ordering).

Similarly for ice/jungle/mushroom/granite tile conversion: vanilla order is `Dither(0.6), Blotches(2,0.6), OnlyTiles(TileType), SetTile...` — Dither first (roll for every tile), then Blotches (2+ rolls per tile!), then OnlyTiles. Ours: checks type first, then rolls. Different consumption AND different ordering.

And Blotches semantics: vanilla Blotches.Apply — when the second roll < chance (0.3 default), it applies the REST of the chain (OnlyTiles + SetTile) over a random rect around the point; the return value `!flag` where flag = any UnitApply failed. Note this means the action can apply OUTSIDE the room rect (x±scale). Also note Blotches consumes 2 rolls even when it doesn't blotch.

OK so the AgeRoom implementation diverges fundamentally:
1. Dither inverted (failureChance vs passChance) — all non-0.5 gates → wrong erosion rates by ~5.7x.
2. Modifier order: vanilla rolls Dither/Blotches for every tile in the room before the type filter; ours filters first → rng consumption differs.
3. Blotches replaced by deterministic coherent noise: different geometry + zero RNG vs 2-6 rolls/tile.
4. Wood's third Gen (OnlyTiles(30,321,158) + ClearTile at 5%) missing entirely.
5. Desert stalactite loop missing (rng only).

These are the biggest findings so far for CaveHousePass. Given the file header claims "1:1" for AgeRoom ("蚀变 = 各主题 AgeRoom override(Dither+Blotches 噪声门禁)"), and the Dither inversion is provably wrong (both 1405 and 1456 agree on `>= failureChance`), this is a must-fix of the highest order.

WorldUtils.Gen applies the chain's Apply per point in the shape. So yes, Dither rolls first for every point.

Ours: `if (activeAt && type===MUSH70 && rng.next() < 0.6)` — rolls only for MUSH70 tiles. Stream + rate deviation (vanilla 40%, ours 60%).

```
if (num > num2) → from = (rectangle.X + rectangle.Width - 1, rectangle.Y + 1), to = (rectangle.X + rectangle.Width - rectangle.Height + 1, rectangle.Y + rectangle.Height - 1)
else → from = (rectangle.X, rectangle.Y + 1), to = (rectangle.X + rectangle.Height - 1, rectangle.Y + rectangle.Height - 1)
```
Ours:
```
const leftGap = prev.x - cur.x; const rightGap = cur.x + cur.w - (prev.x + prev.w);
if (leftGap > rightGap) from = { x: cur.x + cur.w - 1, y: cur.y + 1 }; else from = { x: cur.x, y: cur.y + 1 };
const steps = (cur.y + cur.h - 1) - from.y;
```
Vanilla's stair loop: `for (i = 0; i < item2.Y - item.Y; i++) shapeData.Add(num*(i+1), i)` — i.e., steps = to.Y - from.Y where to.Y = rectangle.Y + rectangle.Height - 1. So steps = (cur.y + cur.h - 1) - from.y ✓ ours matches. The x drift: num*(i+1) where num = ±1 based on `item2.X > item.X` — to.X vs from.X. Vanilla to.X for the first branch = rectangle.X + rectangle.Width - rectangle.Height + 1; from.X = rectangle.X + rectangle.Width - 1. So to.X - from.X = -Height + 2 → negative → num = -1. Ours: `const dir = leftGap > rightGap ? -1 : 1;` ✓ matches (leftGap > rightGap → first branch → dir -1).

OURS: `st.setTileSilent(sx, sy, PLATFORM, 0, 0)` — frame (0,0) = style 0 (wood platform) regardless of theme! Vanilla uses the theme's PlatformStyle. So stairs in ice/jungle/desert/granite/marble/mushroom houses get WOOD platforms in ours. That's a real bug (geometry/cosmetic — wrong platform type). Let me also verify placeSupportBeams: vanilla `CreateSupportBeamList` then PlaceSupportBeams:
- step: `int num3 = 6; while (num3 > 4 && (num2 - num) % num3 != 0) num3--;` ✓ ours.

Ours tryDoor: clears 3 tiles, then `if (solidAt(x, exitY-1) && solidAt(x, exitY+3)) placeDoorClosed(...)`. This is the documented PlaceDoor-semantics approximation (the comment explains mount/floor conditions). Our reconstruction: Branch A fires when j-1, j-2 empty and j-3 solid → door at j-2..j; Branch B fires when j+1..j+2 empty and j+3 solid → door at j..j+2.
